# Plan (priorities)

**This file is the project's single ongoing R&D plan** — the durable, fan-out-ranked backlog of component work. Co-owned by `meta-phase` and `cycle-planner`.

**Intake → plan → dispatch (load-bearing flow; user directive 2026-05-28):**
- **Intake channels are transient, not holding pens.** `scaffolding/open-questions.md`, `scaffolding/friction-ledger.md`, and `problems/` are where issues/friction are *reported*. Items do **not** accumulate there indefinitely.
- **Migration is the resolution.** `meta-phase` (every batch) triages intake and **migrates actionable items INTO this plan** (closing / deferring the rest). `cycle-planner` surfaces fresh-intake plan candidates mid-batch via its `## Open questions / caveats`. An open question or a friction pattern is "resolved" into the plan, not parked.
- **Fan-out ranking drives dispatch.** Every backlog item carries a **fan-out note** — what it unblocks downstream. The planning phase dispatches the **highest-fan-out** work first (use `roadmap.md`'s impact model: `|concepts| × |downstream-reuse| × 1/cost`). This is the *reason* the plan exists: so the planner prioritizes components with the most fan-out impact.
- **Both phases maintain it.** `cycle-planner` reads it every primary cycle to pick dispatches and may append caveats; `meta-phase` reshapes it each batch from intake; `integrator-finalize` removes items as they land.

**Discipline:**
- **Now (active head)** stays focused (≤~10 picks for the current/next cycle).
- **Backlog (fan-out-ranked)** is uncapped — it holds migrated work-items, grouped High/Medium/Low fan-out.
- Each item: slug + one-line rationale + **fan-out:** note (what it unblocks).
- Integrator removes when an item lands in the artifact; meta-phase re-ranks each batch.

> **⟢ BATCH-16 META-PHASE ENACTMENTS (post-c054 finalize; cycles 052/053/054). Decisions:**
> 1. **BATCH-16 ARC ASSESSED — solver-test-load HAS LEGS as the batch-17 frontier (GO).** The arc landed cleanly: refactor-pass-complete (c052: all 12 fold-family leaf chapters reduced to combinator-pointer stubs) → solver-test-load productively underway (c053 probes discharged the single-witness gate + landed 2 `gram` witnesses + opened the FE-assembly thread → c054 mined `solve_family` + promoted `fe_assemble` to firm L1). The solver-test-load is the right batch-17+ frontier — it is generating both genuine new spine vocabulary (`solve_family`, `fe_assemble`) AND spine-coverage findings, exactly the redirect's item-3 intent.
> 2. **BATCH-17 FRONTIER DECIDED (GO — fan-out-ranked below).** Lead = the `solve_family` propagation (replace-and-propagate the mined combinator); then the FE-assembly continuation; then the `map_solve` superset (over-unification-guarded); then the gram-consuming postprocess (deferred-if-too-solver-specific).

The strawman §3.7 `iterate_while` family **IS the shared parent — do NOT author a third combinator as a parent abstraction.** Both are §3.7-family entries; the map-vs-fold distinction is the load-bearing axis between them. This is consistent with the redirect's in-layer-conciseness principle (a new parent abstraction would add vocabulary without simplifying anything — exactly the mine-and-strand anti-pattern). SweepAdaptive (c057 D3) is the *greedy/error-terminated* fold sub-shape (state-derived loop bound AND state-derived per-step input), strictly richer than transient's fixed-step march — a fold-family combinator must not assume the simpler `iterate_while`-with-fixed-schedule shape covers it.

> **⟢⟢ USER DIRECTIVE 2026-06-02 (NEW standing goal, post-batch-21-meta) — FEATURE-SURFACE SPINE: Palace entry points as dedicated feature-surface chapters. (Full def in project memory `project_feature_surface_spine`.)**
> Palace has high-level **entry-point features** — what Palace is *written for*. Even as each decomposes into collections of internal constructions, there must remain **dedicated chapters explaining those entry points as feature surfaces at each level.** A TOP-DOWN feature spine **parallel** to the existing BOTTOM-UP vocabulary spine; a feature chapter is a **composition-root** (inputs = config; outputs = the physical product; body = the composition of the decomposed vocabulary at that level; links DOWN to the constituent ops/combinators). It COMPOSES the vocabulary, does not replace it. A distinct **kind** of chapter (composes with directive-3 → a "feature surfaces / entry points" kind grouping at each layer).
> - **Feature set (user-decided — all four):** (1) the **5 simulation drivers** (electrostatic / magnetostatic / eigenmode / driven / transient — `palace/drivers/*solver`); (2) the **top-level simulation lifecycle** (`main` → `BaseSolver`: config→mesh→assemble→solve→postprocess→output); (3) the **output/postprocess products** (S-parameters / capacitance / inductance / eigenfrequencies+Q / energy-fields) as their own feature surfaces; (4) **wave-port / boundary-mode** (`boundarymodesolver`, the driven-consumed sub-feature).
> - **Levels (user-decided):** **L4 + L1 + L0** each get a dedicated feature-surface chapter (L4 = calculus/backend-lowering feature surface; L1 = pure-function surface; L0 = ground-truth driver source). **L2/L3 feature chapters ONLY where the decomposition meaningfully reshapes** (not mandatory).
> - **Priority (user-decided):** **run in PARALLEL** — a co-equal standing goal; the planner/meta-phase interleave feature-surface chapters with the ongoing bottom-up vocabulary frontier by fan-out (NOT a replacement frontier, NOT necessarily the lead).
> - **Connections:** the L4 feature surfaces ARE the outward backend-lowering entry points (`project_l4_is_backend_lowering_target`; the per-pipeline L4-completeness picture is the 5-driver subset — e.g. the driven-solve→L4 gap (active head #1) lives WITHIN the driven feature). Composes with the directive-3 by-kind grouping (`feedback_mdbook_subchapter_grouping_and_alpha_api`).
> - **Enactment:** the **batch-22 meta-phase codifies** it into role-specs (cycle-planner picks feature work by fan-out; `layer-intro-author`/`harvester` author composition-root feature chapters — NO new agent) + CLAUDE.md §"Extraction goal" gains the feature-spine description + the directive-3 kind list gains a "feature surfaces / entry points" kind. Until codified, the cycle-planner/orchestrator carries it via per-dispatch prompts (cycle-070+). Active head #6 below is the first parallel pick.

## CYCLE-067 / batch-21 active head (reshaped by the batch-20 meta-phase, post-cycle-066 finalize — the FE-space sub-spine is at 3 firm members with the consumer re-anchor complete at both layers; batch-21 finishes the FE-space sub-spine tail, then weighs the next in-scope direction)

> **⟢⟢ USER DIRECTIVE 2026-06-02 — L4 IS THE FEATURE SURFACE AND THE OUTWARD BACKEND-LOWERING TARGET. (Answers the batch-20 meta-phase §5 strategic ASK; SUPERSEDES its "(b) propagate to L2/L3, per-entry-warrant-gated" framing for the FE cohort. Full rationale in project memory `project_l4_is_backend_lowering_target`.)**
> L4's semantics were deliberately chosen to align with the **external** semantics of GPU tensor-accelerator systems + distributed-architecture systems — L4 is the target because it is **ideal for lowering to other backends** (burn/GPU/distributed). This is an *outward* lowering (L4 → external backend), distinct from the internal L4→L3→…→L0 provenance chain. **Consequence: L4 must be COMPLETE over the in-scope feature set** — a backend lowering from L4 that lacks vocabulary for an in-scope feature cannot express it.
> - **The solve half reaches L4** (`ksp_solve`/`eigsolve`/`solve_family`/`fold_solve`/`krylov-step`/`chebyshev`/`iterate-while`). **The assemble half does NOT** — the FE-assembly + FE-space cohort (`fe_assemble`, `fe_space`, `fe_collection`, `essential_dofs`, `weak_form_term`, `eliminate_*`, `assemble_frequency_operator`) is **stranded at L1 with zero presence above L1** (verified 2026-06-02 grep). This is a real hole in the deliverable.
> - **BATCH-21 DIRECTION (decided): after the (a) FE-space sub-spine tail cleanup, the lead is to LIFT THE FE-ASSEMBLY/FE-SPACE COHORT TO L4** — an `assemble`-fold combinator (`fe_assemble` is literally a concatenation-homomorphism fold → natural L4 combinator shape) + construction terms (`fe_space`/`fe_collection`/`essential_dofs`), wrapping the opaque libCEED/MFEM per-element leaves as `readonly` inputs — the SAME opaque-leaf-wrapping pattern `fold_solve`/`eigsolve` already use at L4. Target = **L4**, not L2/L3.
> - **The anti-mirror NO-L2 warrants (`fe_assemble` c063, `weak_form_term`, `fold_solve` c060, `essential_dofs`, `L2/fe_assemble`) govern ONLY the L1→L2 rectangular-MIRROR question — they do NOT close the upward-to-L4 question.** Intermediate L2/L3 rungs may legitimately be identity/skipped (use the cycle-012 non-adjacent identity-rotation in-line convention); L4 is the required destination regardless. Do NOT read "no L2 mirror" as "upward climb done." The STOP-PROPOSING entries below stay valid as written (they bar L2 *mirrors*); they do not bar L4 forms.
> - **RE-EXAMINE the "no-L4-by-design" calls under this directive** — esp. the L4-index "13-of-18 BLAS-1/elementwise/smoother L3 ops = no-L4-by-design" line. Nuance to JUDGE per-case (not blanket-overturn): base tensor primitives already in L3 *global-tensor-field* form may be the backend-target form for primitives, whereas feature-coordination/assembly vocabulary needs the L4 form. Surface as an early-batch-21 cross-cutter/abstractor question.
> - The c067 planner reshapes the active head below accordingly: keep item-1 (FE-space tail cleanup) as the immediate clean lead, then promote the **FE-cohort→L4 lift** to THE batch-21 frontier (was item-3 candidate (b), now decided + retargeted L2/L3→L4). Items (c)/(d) stay lower-priority candidates.

> **⟢⟢ USER DIRECTIVE 2026-06-02 (companion) — BLACK-BOX KERNELS vs ACCELERATED KERNELS (two opposite dispositions at the bottom of the stack). Full def in project memory `project_blackbox_vs_accelerated_kernels`; author a `book/src/concepts/` page early in batch-21 (layer-intro-author).**
> - **Black-box operation kernel — RISES to L4.** A tensor op with **no easy decomposition but a clean operation surface**, usually heavy **non-local iterative value exploration**. Declaring one is PERMITTED when necessary to lift it (no straightforward impl) — it is a first-class primitive, NOT a failure. Its clean surface rises to L4; body stays opaque (the external backend supplies the impl). Examples: `eigsolve` (canonical), `ksp_solve`, the per-element libCEED quadrature leaf inside `fe_assemble`, `fold_solve`'s `ode->Step`. **Positive reframe** of the current negative `obstruction (opaque-library-ownership)` / `sequential-obstruction` filing (distinct from an unimplemented enum-only Palace stub, which stays a true obstruction).
> - **Accelerated (special-case) kernel — STOPPED LOW; the combinator rises.** A named op existing **SOLELY to speed a common operation that DOES decompose, with NO standalone abstraction value** — a perf-fused special case of a general combinator. Disposition: identify LOW (L1/L0), tie to the harvested combinator, **prevent from rising** — the combinator rises in its place. Candidate (per-case): the `axpy`/`axpby`/`axpbypcz`/`scal` fused-update family over `linear_combination`.
> - **Named abstraction — KEEP-and-RISE (even though it decomposes).** *(USER REFINEMENT 2026-06-02.)* Do NOT remove an abstraction just because it has a replaceable kernel / decomposes into a simple combinator application. If the simple named definition is **well-studied / literature-standard AND aids the simplification of other algorithms + their tie-back to the literature**, it is KEPT and RISES (to L4, as a named verb), kernel tied below — alongside its parent combinator (a permitted, genuinely-distinct dual). **Confirmed keeps: `dot` (inner product) + `nrm2` (2-norm)** — wanted as named units ("residual `nrm2(r)`", "`dot(p, Ap)`") in CG/GMRES descriptions, not inlined `inner_product` applications.
> - **Test (three-way, judgment not mechanical):** (1) no decomposition + clean surface → **rises (black-box)**; (2) decomposes AND literature-standard + simplifies downstream → **keep-and-rise (named abstraction)**; (3) decomposes AND solely-for-speed, no abstraction value → **stopped-low (accelerated)**. The decompose-or-not test alone does NOT decide (2)-vs-(3); abstraction value does.
> - **Live consequences for batch-21:** (i) the "no-L4-by-design" BLAS-1 question is answered PER-CASE — a *pure* accelerated kernel correctly gets no L4 chapter, but a *kept named abstraction* (`dot`/`nrm2`) DOES rise to L4; in both cases **`linear_combination`/`inner_product` (the combinators) must RISE to L4** (they currently stop at L3); (ii) **`dot`/`nrm2` are KEEPS** (NOT over-risen — that was an over-correction); the existing `axpy`-family L2+L3 chapters are the per-case demote-vs-keep judgment (weigh literature + downstream-simplification value, NOT just "it decomposes"); the 2026-06-01 leaf-collapse refactor is "keep-vs-stop per-case", not blanket demotion; (iii) the **FE-cohort→L4 lift** uses both halves — the assemble *fold* is the combinator (rises), the per-element FE quadrature leaf is a *black-box kernel* (rises as an opaque-surface input). The c067 planner/early-batch-21 cross-cutter classifies each opaque/special op by the three-way test (weighing abstraction value) before lifting.

## CYCLE-049 / batch-15 active head (reshaped by the 2026-06-01 VOCABULARY-SHIFT REDIRECT — supersedes the batch-14 meta-phase's "L4-complete / strategic-pivot ASK" framing)

> **⟢ 2026-06-01 VOCABULARY-SHIFT REDIRECT (user directive; full spec `METHODOLOGY-REDIRECT.md`, distilled in CLAUDE.md §Methodology invariants).** The "rectangular projection up the stack" the foundation campaign optimized for was an accident of lowering-verification convenience, not the goal. The stack is a sequence of **representational + vocabulary shifts**; each layer is concise **in itself** (conciseness drives in-layer combinators); each lowering is a **translation across vocabularies**, not a rename (a degenerate identity lowering is a smell). **Batch-15 program, in priority order:**
>
> 1. **REFACTOR PASS (highest priority).** Collapse the cycles-041–048 base-form L2/L3 leaf floors into **in-layer combinators** (`axpy`/`axpby`/`axpbypcz`/`scal` → `linear_combination`; `dot`/`nrm2` → `inner_product`; etc. as the combinator-miner identifies families), **propagate the combinator upward** (L3/L4 express *through* it, not re-derived base form), **demote the thin `-body-identity` L3>L2 / L2>L1 themes** to in-line notes, and re-audit every touched lowering under the translation/smell test. Route `combinator-miner` (identify family + replace + propagate plan) → `harvester`/`abstractor`/`lifter` (enact). This precedes new forward-frontier work — the corrected shape is the foundation.
> 2. **Continued shared-spine abstraction under the corrected model** (combinator-miner replace-and-propagate as conciseness pressure surfaces families).
> 3. **SOLVERS AS LOW-PRIORITY TEST-LOAD.** Begin pulling the 5 pipelines (electrostatic/magnetostatic/eigenmode/driven/transient) + FE assembly up through the layers — LOW priority, never preempting spine work; a solver advances a layer ONLY when cleanly describable in existing shared vocabulary; what it can't cleanly say is a finding fed back as spine work. Never force/distort the spine to advance a solver.

No producer-spec bullet imposed — paraphrase aids readability and is bounded by the line-range containment guard (the auditor self-discloses the paraphrase, the critic verifies the range contains the semantics). ## Methodology guidance (user directive 2026-05-27; cycle-003 + cycle-004 update)

- **Dispatch target: up to 12 sub-agents per cycle** (user directive 2026-05-27 — raised from 8 mid-cycle-006 after cycle-005 first-validation of the split integrator showed per-dispatch context stays bounded regardless of wave-mate count). Trajectory: initial 15 target → 8 at cycle-004 → cycle-005 boundary (integrator-token-budget concern at 7 wave-mates pre-split) → 12 mid-cycle-006 (post-split-validation re-expansion). Cycle-004 ran **7 wave-1 dispatches** with zero structural conflict at integration (5 wave-mates appending to `book/src/L1/index.md` cleanly). Cycle-005 ran 6 wave-1 dispatches under the split integrator with zero per-dispatch context-bound friction. Cycle-007+ target: **up to 12 dispatches**. See `.claude/agents/cycle-planner.md` Discipline section.
- **Integrator-to-planner signals channel**: integrator appends to `scaffolding/integrator-signals.md` each cycle (newest at top). Cycle-planner reads the top ~3 entries each cycle. **Validated cycles 003–004** (friction-ledger entry `integrator-signals-channel-working-as-designed`, recurrence-2). 5 of cycle-004's 7 dispatches were sourced verbatim from cycle-003's Suggested next dispatches.
- **`verified_against:` YAML must be fenced** (cycle-003 meta-phase): lowering-verifier emissions of the `verified_against:` block must use a fenced ` ```yaml ... ``` ` code block. See `.claude/agents/lowering-verifier.md` Discipline + friction-ledger entry `lowering-verifier-yaml-in-prose-channel-format`.
- **`layer-intro-author` role broadened** (cycle-003 meta-phase): covers `book/src/concepts/<slug>.md` authorship in addition to layer intros. Cycle-004 first use: `concepts/dot.md` rewrite landed cleanly. See `.claude/agents/layer-intro-author.md`.
- **`layer-intro-author` Vocabulary-cohort subsection template** (cycle-004 meta-phase): when a layer's `index.md` accumulates ≥3 firm operators + ≥1 queued (rough-in / obstruction), include a Vocabulary-cohort subsection that splits the dep-map by firmness state. Template documented in `.claude/agents/layer-intro-author.md` §Vocabulary-cohort subsection. Originated cycle-004 in L1 intro; promote to L2/L3/L4 as those reach the threshold.
- **`addressed-by-design` is NOT a workaround-active category** (cycle-004 meta-phase, user-escalation derived): if a status's mitigation couples orchestration to a quirk, escalate to user; do not file under `addressed-by-design`. See friction-ledger entry `addressed-by-design-misuse-as-workaround-silting`. Meta-phase audits surviving `addressed-by-design` entries per cycle via the counterfactual "if this quirk were removed, what process changes would I make?"
- **REPORT.md → CYCLE.md naming convention** (cycle-004 user-directive rename, commit 8ac1f37): all per-dispatch report files are named `CYCLE.md`. Content-pattern Write filter is bypassed; subagents Write directly. Skill `embed-and-persist-subagent-dispatch` is retired (kept as historical). Watch-list note: monitor other Claude Code projects for the same filter and apply the same repair pattern if it recurs.
